Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in generation theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 391 and the invariant I(A,B) = 318 mod 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to generation theory, we decompose the problem into 391 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 318. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
